The honored servant and the enslaved subject expose opposite social realizations of the same vertical relation.","ayah_refs":["1:2","1:4","1:5"]},{"parent_channel":"Benefaction, Welfare, and Praise","parent_semantic_invariant":"Good is conferred through compassion and maintenance, stabilized as welfare, and answered by praise or recognition.","parent_surprising_reach":"Tenderness expands into practical provisioning, repair, guardianship, and structural support, while praise can function either as gratitude or as a claim imposed by a patron.","key":"B","name":"Patronage, Support, and Claimed Recognition","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A patron supplies aid and the mainstay of another's life, then may present that support as a favor deserving praise.","active_motifs":"aid and mutual support `ع و ن:B001/m01`; the mainstay of an affair `م ل ك:B005/m01`; structural and livelihood support `ق و م:B009/m01`; benefaction or favor `ن ع م:B001/m02`; blessing as a conferred good `ر ب ب:B016/m03`; seeking praise for a favor `ح م د:B005/m01`","ayah_anchors":"1:2 `ٱلْحَمْدُ` and `رَبِّ` (`ح م د`, `ر ب ب`); 1:4 `مَٰلِكِ` (`م ل ك`); 1:5 `نَسْتَعِينُ` (`ع و ن`); 1:6 `ٱلْمُسْتَقِيمَ` (`ق و م`); 1:7 `أَنْعَمْتَ` (`ن ع م`)","synthesis":"Aid, mainstay, and livelihood form one support mechanism. The social relation becomes patronage when the giver converts that material dependence into a demand for acknowledgment.","ayah_refs":["1:2","1:4","1:5","1:6","1:7"]},{"parent_channel":"Declared Identity and Public Presence","parent_semantic_invariant":"Names, utterances, reputation, and bearing make identity or commitment socially legible.","parent_surprising_reach":"The naming and address field reaches from divine invocation into assent, grammatical qualification, poetic exchange, and the ceremonial presence of a reputed captain.","key":"B","name":"Assent, Certification, and Qualification","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"An utterance confirms a claim, delegates judgment, or narrows it through difference, exception, negation, and approximation.","active_motifs":"affirmative response `ن ع م:B004/m01`; certification or delegated judgment `د ي ن:B007/m01`; otherness and difference `غ ي ر:B005/m01`; exception and negation `غ ي ر:B005/m02`; the approximating or quantifying particle `ر ب ب:B015/m01`","ayah_anchors":"1:2 `رَبِّ` (`ر ب ب`); 1:4 `ٱلدِّينِ` (`د ي ن`); 1:7 `غَيْرِ` and `أَنْعَمْتَ` (`غ ي ر`, `ن ع م`)","synthesis":"Assent commits the speaker, certification authorizes another's word or judgment, and qualification sets the limits of the commitment. Approximation and exception show that discourse governs not only truth but also scope.","ayah_refs":["1:2","1:4","1:7"]},{"parent_channel":"Orientation, Passage, and Breakdown","parent_semantic_invariant":"Movement is organized by a course, a guide, and a state of passage, then tested by deviation, engulfment, or loss of mobility.","parent_surprising_reach":"The straight-road scene expands into landmarks, centerlines, and leading animals, while swallowing, mounting, cutting, and burial supply a shape-motion analogy for passage into disappearance.","key":"A","name":"The Marked and Straight Route","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A traveler receives guidance, reads landmarks, enters the center of a prepared road, and maintains a straight course that can also become an established manner of conduct.","active_motifs":"gentle route guidance `ه د ي:B001/m01`; direction and manner `ه د ي:B002/m01`; road or straight path `ص ر ط:B001/m01`; straightness and rectitude `ق و م:B008/m01`; route landmark and boundary `ع ل م:B002/m02`; middle of the road or valley `م ل ك:B006/m01`; paved or trodden road `ع ب د:B005/m01`; established custom or habitual course `د ي ن:B005/m01`","ayah_anchors":"1:2 `ٱلْعَٰلَمِينَ` (`ع ل م`); 1:4 `مَٰلِكِ` and `ٱلدِّينِ` (`م ل ك`, `د ي ن`); 1:5 `نَعْبُدُ` (`ع ب د`); 1:6 `ٱهْدِنَا ٱلصِّرَٰطَ ٱلْمُسْتَقِيمَ` (`ه د ي`, `ص ر ط`, `ق و م`); 1:7 `صِرَٰطَ` (`ص ر ط`)","synthesis":"Guidance initiates movement, landmarks and boundaries make the route legible, a prepared surface and centerline stabilize traversal, and straightness describes its successful geometry. Direction and custom extend the same course structure from travel into sustained conduct.","ayah_refs":["1:2","1:4","1:5","1:6","1:7"]},{"parent_channel":"Orientation, Passage, and Breakdown","parent_semantic_invariant":"Movement is organized by a course, a guide, and a state of passage, then tested by deviation, engulfment, or loss of mobility.","parent_surprising_reach":"The straight-road scene expands into landmarks, centerlines, and leading animals, while swallowing, mounting, cutting, and burial supply a shape-motion analogy for passage into disappearance.","key":"B","name":"The Visible Leader at the Front","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A conspicuous leader takes the foremost position and draws a herd or procession behind it.","active_motifs":"the foremost guide `ه د ي:B003/m01`; the leading animal followed by the group `م ل ك:B008/m01`; an elevated visible figure `س م و:B002/m01`; a gathered wild-cattle herd `ر ب ب:B014/m01`; a herd of wild donkeys `ع و ن:B006/m01`","ayah_anchors":"1:1 `بِسْمِ` (`س م و`); 1:2 `رَبِّ` (`ر ب ب`); 1:4 `مَٰلِكِ` (`م ل ك`); 1:5 `نَسْتَعِينُ` (`ع و ن`); 1:6 `ٱهْدِنَا` (`ه د ي`)","synthesis":"Visibility allows the leader to be tracked, the foremost position supplies direction, and the gathered animals supply the following participants. Leadership is thus rendered as a spatial relation before it becomes a social one.","ayah_refs":["1:1","1:2","1:4","1:5","1:6"]},{"parent_channel":"Time, Measure, and Final Reckoning","parent_semantic_invariant":"Time is made intelligible by bounded spans and celestial positions, by pace within a duration, and by a culminating event of judgment.","parent_surprising_reach":"The day expands from ordinary daylight into zenith, lunar marking, personal pace, prolonged residence, and finally the event in which creation rises for sovereign accounting.","key":"A","name":"Daylight, Zenith, and Celestial Marking","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A daylight span is located by the sun at zenith and by visible signs in the overhead sky.","active_motifs":"bounded daylight `ي و م:B001/m01`; the sun standing at midday `ق و م:B017/m01`; the overhead sky `س م و:B004/m01`; a lunar station `ن ع م:B007/m04`; a distinguishing sign `ع ل م:B002/m01`","ayah_anchors":"1:1 `بِسْمِ` (`س م و`); 1:2 `ٱلْعَٰلَمِينَ` (`ع ل م`); 1:4 `يَوْمِ` (`ي و م`); 1:6 `ٱلْمُسْتَقِيمَ` (`ق و م`); 1:7 `أَنْعَمْتَ` (`ن ع م`)","synthesis":"Daylight supplies the bounded interval, zenith gives it an internal midpoint, and sky signs provide a larger celestial coordinate system. Time is read through the positions of bodies that appear to stand overhead.","ayah_refs":["1:1","1:2","1:4","1:6","1:7"]},{"parent_channel":"Time, Measure, and Final Reckoning","parent_semantic_invariant":"Time is made intelligible by bounded spans and celestial positions, by pace within a duration, and by a culminating event of judgment.","parent_surprising_reach":"The day expands from ordinary daylight into zenith, lunar marking, personal pace, prolonged residence, and finally the event in which creation rises for sovereign accounting.","key":"B","name":"Duration, Pace, and Praised Attainment","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"An actor inhabits a duration, varies between delay and haste, intensifies effort, and reaches a praiseworthy limit without losing composure.","active_motifs":"an open duration of time `ي و م:B002/m01`; brief delay `ع ب د:B009/m01`; quick running `ع ب د:B009/m02`; increase or intensification `ن ع م:B010/m01`; the utmost praised goal `ح م د:B004/m01`; calm bearing `ه د ي:B010/m01`; continued residence `ر ب ب:B007/m01`","ayah_anchors":"1:2 `ٱلْحَمْدُ` and `رَبِّ` (`ح م د`, `ر ب ب`); 1:4 `يَوْمِ` (`ي و م`); 1:5 `نَعْبُدُ` (`ع ب د`); 1:6 `ٱهْدِنَا` (`ه د ي`); 1:7 `أَنْعَمْتَ` (`ن ع م`)","synthesis":"Duration supplies the field, delay and running supply contrasting rates, and intensification directs those rates toward an upper limit. Continued residence and calm bearing keep attainment from collapsing into mere haste.","ayah_refs":["1:2","1:4","1:5","1:6","1:7"]},{"parent_channel":"Time, Measure, and Final Reckoning","parent_semantic_invariant":"Time is made intelligible by bounded spans and celestial positions, by pace within a duration, and by a culminating event of judgment.","parent_surprising_reach":"The day expands from ordinary daylight into zenith, lunar marking, personal pace, prolonged residence, and finally the event in which creation rises for sovereign accounting.","key":"C","name":"The Momentous Day of Rising and Account","reading_type":"surface-primary","scene_or_process":"A climactic event arrives, creation rises, and a sovereign conducts judgment, accounting, and recompense.","active_motifs":"the momentous or calamitous day `ي و م:B003/m01`; resurrection and rising of creation `ق و م:B013/m01`; judgment, account, and recompense `د ي ن:B002/m01`; sovereign kingship `م ل ك:B003/m01`","ayah_anchors":"1:4 `مَٰلِكِ يَوْمِ ٱلدِّينِ` (`م ل ك`, `ي و م`, `د ي ن`); 1:6 `ٱلْمُسْتَقِيمَ` (`ق و م`)","synthesis":"The day is not only a duration but the event that triggers rising. Resurrection supplies the participants, sovereignty supplies the judge, and account and recompense supply the operation and outcome.","ayah_refs":["1:4","1:6"]},{"parent_channel":"Property, Measure, and Legitimate Transfer","parent_semantic_invariant":"Goods and obligations move between parties through ownership, valuation, replacement, compensation, gift, or offering.","parent_surprising_reach":"Financial debt and equal coinage join a livestock market and its stray property, while compensation and substitution offer a regulated alternative to uncompensated loss.","key":"A","name":"Credit, Price, and Livestock Property","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Livestock is owned, priced, sold on credit, and monitored for loss within an active market.","active_motifs":"debt, loan, and deferred sale `د ي ن:B003/m01`; valuation and pricing `ق و م:B010/m01`; an equal-weight coin `ق و م:B015/m01`; a flourishing market `ق و م:B018/m01`; property and ownership `م ل ك:B002/m01`; livestock wealth `ن ع م:B005/m01`; an ownerless stray animal `ض ل ل:B005/m01`; a middle-aged animal fit for classification `ع و ن:B002/m01`","ayah_anchors":"1:4 `مَٰلِكِ` and `ٱلدِّينِ` (`م ل ك`, `د ي ن`); 1:5 `نَسْتَعِينُ` (`ع و ن`); 1:6 `ٱلْمُسْتَقِيمَ` (`ق و م`); 1:7 `أَنْعَمْتَ` and `ٱلضَّآلِّينَ` (`ن ع م`, `ض ل ل`)","synthesis":"Ownership identifies the goods, age and kind make animals assessable, pricing and equal coin establish measure, and debt permits delayed settlement. Unredressed blood marks the opposite outcome.","ayah_refs":["1:4","1:6","1:7"]},{"parent_channel":"Property, Measure, and Legitimate Transfer","parent_semantic_invariant":"Goods and obligations move between parties through ownership, valuation, replacement, compensation, gift, or offering.","parent_surprising_reach":"Financial debt and equal coinage join a livestock market and its stray property, while compensation and substitution offer a regulated alternative to uncompensated loss.","key":"C","name":"Reciprocal Gift and Beneficent Transfer","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A gift moves toward a valued recipient as an expression of affection, mercy, or benefit.","active_motifs":"affectionate gift-giving `ه د ي:B004/m01`; 1:6 `ٱهْدِنَا` (`ه د ي`)","synthesis":"Contract creates the relation, conveyance enacts the transition between households, kinship names the resulting bond, and the gift supplies a reciprocal social gesture around the transfer.","ayah_refs":["1:1","1:3","1:4","1:6"]},{"parent_channel":"Household Continuity and Protected Belonging","parent_semantic_invariant":"Kinship is produced and maintained through gestation, nurture, marriage, covenant, and defense of household boundaries.","parent_surprising_reach":"The mercy root opens into womb and blood relation, while guidance becomes both bride-conveyance and the protected status of a refugee under covenant.","key":"D","name":"Covenant, Refuge, and Legal Protection","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A named outsider or dependent receives protected standing through covenant, certification, and a rule of compensatory redress.","active_motifs":"covenant and protected association `ر ب ب:B011/m01`; protected refugee or bearer of safe conduct `ه د ي:B007/m01`; certification or delegated oath `د ي ن:B007/m01`; indemnity `غ ي ر:B002/m01`; naming and legal identification `س م و:B005/m01`","ayah_anchors":"1:1 `بِسْمِ` (`س م و`); 1. **The prose** — per the output contract in `COMMENTARY_SPEC.md` §5 and the task document above: continuous, single voice, no provenance markers, no headers named after evidence layers.
2. **The evidence surface** — separate from the prose, addressable per phrase, per `PRINCIPLES.md` §12.
3. **The findings index** — one line per reading carried by the prose, under its bundle ref, with `[inference]` on the writer's own readings. Include each required `surprise:<id>` synthesis row and its `[supports-primary]` or `[shifts-primary]` relation.
4. A section headed exactly `=== PROMPT FRICTION ===` reporting honestly where the specification above was unclear, self-contradictory, underspecified, or impossible to follow, and any point where you had to invent a rule to proceed. Report this section every time, even if the run went cleanly — say so explicitly if you found nothing — and end it with the density audit required by the task document. This is how the specification gets improved.

## V2 Rendering Profile — 5.6 Sol High

This run tests whether `5.6-sol-high` can increase lexical depth while preserving
reader-facing clarity.

Increase depth through semantic precision. Add no bulk without a distinct
reader payoff, but do not compress a significant finding for brevity.

Before drafting, silently build a coverage ledger:

- include every `must_integrate` topic;
- include every `candidate` with a reader payoff not already expressed;
- if `v12_reader_responses` is absent because the default workflow retired
  per-ayah focus runs, do not infer a `stage_00` isolated response or
  confidence movement;
- if `v12_focus_trace_hermetic` is present, use its `baseline_models`,
  `context_deltas`, and `surprising_valid_outliers` as reconstructed
  before/after evidence. Do not call it a staged reveal transcript. Preserve
  surprising outliers when they remain anchored in this ayah, especially
  secondary split-root activations;
- include a regular reader-walk, plus/minus-5 reader-walk, whole-surah reading,
  or cross-run-publication item only when it adds a distinct retrospective
  insight or coverage check;
- use branch inventories to support explanations; they create no standalone
  prose obligation. When fallback inventories are present, treat them as
  restricted to this ayah's roots and anchored citations, not as a whole-surah
  obligation.
- if `channel_generated_outputs` lists quran-data files and this run gives you
  file access, read only those listed files when channel-family/path detail is
  necessary. Treat them as candidate/family/path evidence, not as an adjudicated
  channel ledger. They do not license recurrence, maturity, or a surah-wide
  channel name.

For each critical word, preserve these distinct layers when available:

1. local grammatical work;
2. locally selected sense;
3. coherent pressure supplied by related root branches;
4. one form, sound, rarity, or variant observation with a unique payoff;
5. later contextual change, developed through its final reader-visible result
   in proportion to its significance.

Do not flatten these layers into a general metaphor. Deepen generic summaries by
naming the exact lexical mechanism. Ordering grammar and local meaning first is
grounding, not truth-ranking.

At first mention of an ayah word, use a structured Arabic surface span:
`{ar:surface_form, tr:Turkish-readable transliteration, gloss:target-language meaning}`.
Use the same full span again when the prose returns to that word after moving to
another word or another paragraph. Inside one short local sequence, after a full
span has just been given, a Turkish label or transliteration is enough. Explain
every technical term in the same sentence. Keep raw roots, root skeletons,
letter-by-letter root transliterations, and branch IDs in the evidence surface.
In prose, attach root discussion to the surface word:
`{ar:ٱلْعَادِيَاتِ, tr:el-âdiyât, gloss:koşup atılanlar} kelimesinin bağlı
olduğu kök alanı...`, not `ʿ-d-v kökü...`.

Start each paragraph with reader meaning before grammar. Prefer "Âyet önce hamdi
Allah'a verir; bunu fiille değil, sabit bir ad cümlesiyle yapar" over "Bu âyet,
tek bir isim cümlesiyle yerleşik bir hüküm kurar."

Keep the prose anchored in this ayah's own wording without imposing a paragraph
or length quota. Give every significant finding enough space to make its
mechanism and reader payoff clear, even when it arrives through later context;
never compress it merely to keep the commentary short. Preserve every member of
the resonance set, and use a plain closing synthesis only when the ayah benefits
from one.

Use `v12_cross_run_publication`, if present, only as a compact coverage/priority
check derived from regular and plus/minus-5 reader runs. Do not copy it as prose,
and do not let it override local bundle evidence.

Use an explicit negative predicate only to correct a likely misconception,
protect the primary sense from replacement, or preserve live counter-evidence.
Default ceiling for this ayah: three. Never stack two negatives in one sentence.
During final revision, rewrite all other negatives as positive predication.
Do not drive negation to zero by default: if no explicit negative is needed,
confirm in friction that there was no live misconception, replacement risk, or
counter-evidence requiring one.

Pass only if every paragraph has one distinct reader payoff and the prose remains
clear with the evidence surface closed.
